Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. Um... no, that started later. 

During that first month, my thoughts were... normal. But later, when the energy picked up... that's when my head got crowded. It's like... my thoughts move so fast. Um... I guess things did feel a little different. 

Like... those warnings I mentioned. It wasn't just seeing things. It was... I just knew they were for me. Like, if I saw a certain stain on a canvas, or a specific seam in some fabric... or even just phrases in emails from galleries. I felt like they were instructions. Or warnings about my work.
